This implies that the camera is positioned in a way to capture events happening on or near the lake.

This proposition provides insight into the camera's setup and its potential to capture the lake area.

The camera was intended for a meteor shower but ended up taking a crucial picture related to the crime.

**Step 5:** Given the information and the analysis framework, the contradiction lies between the intended use of the camera for a meteor shower and its actual use in capturing a crime scene. This contradiction is best represented by the evidence of the camera and the testimony about its intended use.

**Step 2:** Check if any spatial propositions from the list above apply to this contradiction

This discrepancy between the locked door (as per testimony) and the door being jammed from the inside (as per evidence) presents a spatial contradiction regarding how Lance Amano escaped.
